56	"// A reloadable component is an update boundary wherever it renders, so each row"	comment.line.pw
57	"// is its own fragment with its own id. Two things follow, and both are visible"	comment.line.pw
58	"// in the network tab: the browser can ask for one row again without touching"	comment.line.pw
59	"// the rest of the page, and a search that only reorders the rows leaves the"	comment.line.pw
60	"// ones it keeps alone."	comment.line.pw
61	"//"	comment.line.pw
62	"// Its parameters arrive from whoever issued the redraw, which is why a"	comment.line.pw
63	"// component that loaded a record by identifier would have to check ownership"	comment.line.pw
64	"// itself. This one formats values handed to it, which is the shape that is safe"	comment.line.pw
65	"// to publish without a check."	comment.line.pw
